【英媒：X平臺成極右翼製造輿論對立的武器】英國媒體《衛報》9日報道，關於英國6月發生的兩起騷亂事件，美國企業家馬斯克旗下社交媒體平臺X上流量最高的帖文有相當數量源自美國賬號，該平臺已成為外國極右翼勢力製造輿論對立的武器。6月8日，英國北愛爾蘭首府貝爾法斯特發生一起惡性持刀襲擊事件，嫌疑人為一名蘇丹裔男子。案發後，記錄襲擊過程的影片在社交媒體上廣泛傳播，極右翼人士煽風點火，引發巨大震動。貝爾法斯特隨後發生“打砸搶”，至少12名警員受傷。同月，在英格蘭南部城市南安普敦，也發生一起嫌疑人為印度裔的兇殺案，當地反移民情緒被點燃，爆發大規模騷亂。在這兩起事件後的輿論動盪中，社交媒體均起到推波助瀾的作用。《衛報》的分析資料顯示，在X平臺釋出兩地騷亂內容、且單帖瀏覽量破千的8672個賬號中，45.3%的網路IP地址在英國，16.8%位於美國；瀏覽量最高的12條帖文中有7條來自美國賬號，其中一條由馬斯克本人發出。馬斯克在那條帖文中寫道：“過時的主流媒體，那些寫喬治·弗洛伊德寫了數百萬次的媒體，而今就諾瓦克（遇害）一聲不吭。”這條帖文的瀏覽量超過2800萬次。（新華社）

South Korean Won Eases From Over 10-Month High — The South Korean won eased to around 1,415 per dollar, pulling back slightly from a recent over ten-month high, as renewed uncertainty over the Strait of Hormuz weighed on the currency. Iran denied holding direct talks with Washington over reopening the waterway, casting doubt on a near-term deal and keeping oil prices elevated, raising concerns over import costs and inflation in South Korea. Meanwhile, strong semiconductor exports and continued dollar selling by exporters provided support, with July exports rising nearly 63% year-on-year to $98.89 billion and semiconductor shipments surging 179%. Expectations of foreign exchange intervention by South Korean authorities are also helping limit renewed won weakness and keep the currency near its strongest level since October 2025. At the same time, investors are monitoring this week's US inflation data for further clues on the Federal Reserve's policy outlook and the dollar's direction.

Dutch Manufacturing Output Falls in June — Manufacturing production in the Netherlands fell 1.3% month-on-month in June 2026, following an upwardly revised 1.1% increase in May. This marked the first decline in manufacturing output since February, with production falling mainly in food (-4.8% vs. 1.0% in May), leather and footwear (-4.1% vs. 2.6%), electrical appliances (-1.9% vs. 0.5%), and machinery (-2.9% vs. 7.0%). Output also continued to decline in textiles (-0.2% vs. -0.5%), paper (-0.7% vs. -2.2%), rubber and plastics (-0.4% vs. -1.0%), and metal products (-1.6% vs. -2.4%). On a yearly basis, manufacturing production increased 4.6%, easing from a 6.8% rise in May. Output grew in nearly half of the underlying sectors, with the machinery industry once again recording the largest increase, while machine repair and installation posted the biggest decline.

Sensex Begins Week Lower — India’s BSE Sensex fell about 0.2% to 78,348 on Monday, extending losses from the previous session as investors weighed rising oil prices against easing expectations for a near-term US Federal Reserve rate hike. Investor sentiment received some support from weaker-than-expected US jobs data, which reduced expectations of an imminent Fed Reserve rate hike. The positive global backdrop was partly offset by higher crude prices. Brent crude rose around $84.5 a barrel as uncertainty over the reopening of the Strait of Hormuz continued. Traders will also be watching how the new closing auction behaves as the market enters another week of trading. Among stocks, Titan will be in focus after a sharp rise in quarterly profit, while Dr. Reddy’s ended its distribution agreement with Novartis India. Oil India’s profit more than tripled, while Ola Electric narrowed its first-quarter loss. Top losers were State Bank of India (-1.6%), Kaynes (-1.5%), Cupid (-0.5%), and Hindalco (-0.5%).

【CPI溫和上漲！紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）近5年回報超62％ 機構建議作為底倉配置】8月10日，A股三大指數分化震盪，截至午間收盤，滬指漲0.20％，創指跌2.18％。在此背景下，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）漲0.86％，報1.168元，換手率1.52％，半日成交額4.92億元，居同類標的ETF首位。訊息面上，國家統計局近日釋出資料顯示，7月份居民消費價格指數（CPI）環比下降0.1％，同比上漲0.5％，扣除食品和能源價格的核心CPI環比上漲0.3％，同比上漲0.9％，CPI總體保持溫和上漲；工業生產者出廠價格指數（PPI）環比下降0.7％，同比上漲3.5％，漲幅比6月份回落0.6個百分點。華泰證券表示，展望後市，超跌反彈仍有演繹空間，重點關注創業板指。節奏上，8月中下旬的中報密集期或是檢驗反彈成色的視窗。配置上，錨定中報均衡配置。中期視角下，價效比出海鏈及必需消費處於增配視窗。紅利底倉繼續持有，結構上，重點配置銀行與交運。國信證券指出，A股高景氣行業股價頂部往往呈現“M頂”形態，這一次科技行情或也不例外。M頂第二波機會需要產業層面分歧消除且擁擠度回落，目前AI科技M頂第二波行情的啟動因素正在累積。擾動後A股牛途繼續，結構上均衡佈局，成長或向AI應用擴散，重視紅利，以及基本面較優的醫藥券商、受益政策的內需。投資者可將紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）作為底倉配置，無股票賬戶的投資者也可透過其場外聯接基金進行配置（A類：007466；C類：007467；I類：022678；Y類：022951）。

### 2026-08-10 12:28:57  #其他

【颱風減弱≠影響結束！警惕“三碰頭”疊加風險】“白海豚”雖已減弱為熱帶風暴級，但影響遠未結束。福建省海峽氣象科學研究所副所長、高階工程師陳思提醒，需高度警惕颱風影響期間可能帶來的“三碰頭”疊加風險。何為“三碰頭”？“三碰頭”也叫“風雨潮三碰頭”，指的是颱風帶來的強風、暴雨與天文大潮在相近時間、同一沿海區域疊加出現的極端情況。陳思解釋，風、雨、潮三者本就各有威力，一旦“碰頭”，破壞力將呈幾何級放大。“三碰頭”的危害主要體現為：狂風肆虐，破壞力極強；暴雨傾盆，洪澇風險陡增；潮位頂託，海水倒灌風險高。（央視）

Rupee Holds as RBI Cushions Oil Pressure — The Indian rupee hovered around 95.2 per dollar, holding steady after recent gains as continued Reserve Bank of India intervention and reduced expectations of a September Federal Reserve rate hike provided support. The rupee has been in recovery mode over the past two weeks, aided by regular RBI intervention. Brent crude rose to around $84.50 a barrel on Monday but remained well below its recent high of $100, providing some breathing room for the rupee. The RBI also intervened at the open on Friday, signalling its willingness to prevent oil-price uncertainty from translating into unchecked pressure on the currency. Meanwhile, investors pared bets on a September Fed rate hike after US employers unexpectedly shed 23,000 jobs in July, with Fed funds futures now implying a 44% probability of a hike, down from 55% before the data. The RBI's continued presence in the foreign exchange market has helped cushion the rupee from oil-related pressure and limit downside risks.

### 2026-08-10 11:56:10  #MKT News

【Hainan issues measures to deepen investment-financing reform】Hainan provincial government office issued measures to further reform investment and financing. The plan directs coordination of government funds, policy financial tools and commercial bank lending cadence, and urges policy banks and commercial lenders to join major-project planning earlier. It requires setting a balanced mix of government capital, credit and sponsor own funds and seeks to build a full-cycle capital recycling system—“fund efficiency, investment fund structure, credit-based construction, securitized operations.” Projects with strong returns and broad financing access should see reduced or no government funding. The measures also issue operational guidance to expand a “invest first, convert to equity later” model for science-and-technology fiscal funds; fiscal capital may initially finance commercialization projects (eg, biomedicine, deep-sea technology) and convert to equity once firms mature to share risk and returns.

### 2026-08-10 11:55:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Copper Pulls Back from Record Highs — Copper futures slipped below $6.6 per pound on Monday, retreating from record levels reached last week as traders took profits while weighing signs of tightening global supply. Concerns over potential supply disruptions from the Democratic Republic of Congo’s copper concentrate export ban also eased, with Goldman Sachs saying it expected the measure to have no significant impact on global copper balances. However, traders remained cautious over possible US import tariffs on copper, which continued to redirect metal from international markets into US warehouses. On the demand front, data released over the weekend showed both consumer and producer inflation in top consumer China slowed in July, pointing to persistently weak domestic demand. Data on Friday also showed China’s imports of unwrought copper and copper products dropped 11.5% year on year to 425,000 tonnes in July, while imports for January-July fell 6.2% to 2.92 million tonnes.

【China Satcom joins MIIT IMT-2030 (6G) non‑terrestrial networks working group】The Department of Information and Communication Development at the Ministry of Industry and Information Technology (MIIT) has established the IMT-2030 (6G) promotion group's non‑terrestrial networks (NTN) working group in Beijing. China Satcom was invited as one of the initial member units. The NTN group will steer and support domestic organizations in developing international technical standards, coordinate the overall layout of 6G satellite-terrestrial integrated networks, cultivate core 6G satellite communications technologies, and promote industry‑academia‑research‑use coordination. Its remit covers satellite air-interface access, satellite terminal technologies, satellite-terrestrial network integration, and operations, maintenance and application technologies.

Rupiah Softens After Persistent Decline in Consumer Confidence — The Indonesian rupiah edged lower on Monday, hovering near IDR 17,820 per U.S. dollar after firming to around IDR 17,780 in the prior session. Sentiment was weighed by fresh data showing consumer sentiment fell for a third straight month to a 15-month low in July, amid sustained concerns over current economic conditions and the job outlook. Caution also prevailed ahead of June retail sales data due Tuesday, after sales posted their steepest drop in three years in May due to elevated inflation and higher non-subsidised fuel prices. Still, losses were limited as the U.S. dollar index eased after weaker-than-expected U.S. job data eased bets of further tightening. Locally, foreign investors returned to Indonesian government bonds and equities in July, ending months of sustained selling and providing some support for the rupiah. Traders also remained focused on President Prabowo Subianto’s choice of the next Bank Indonesia governor following Perry Warjiyo’s abrupt departure last month.

【蘋果摺疊iPhone已規劃到第三代，今年全球份額或超華為】8月10日，據知名科技記者馬克·古爾曼在最新一期《Power On》通訊中披露，蘋果已經敲定摺疊iPhone完整三年迭代路線，第三代機型有望最早在2028年推出。古爾曼此前發文稱，蘋果首款摺疊iPhone計劃在2026年秋季正式登場，產品暫定命名為iPhone Ultra，採用書本式向內摺疊方案。硬體層面，初代摺疊iPhone將搭載7.8英寸內屏、5.5英寸外屏，放棄Face ID，改用機身Touch ID，內建A20晶片搭配C2自研基帶，主打超薄機身設計。在定價上，該機起步價格或將達到2000美元，摺合人民幣約13530元，不排除部分版本售價進一步走高。該機構判斷，三星將在2026年繼續保持全球摺疊屏智慧手機市場領先地位，市場份額預計為32%。不過，隨著蘋果正式入局，加之華為（尤其是在中國市場）保持強勁勢頭，2026年的市場格局將與2025年大不相同。機構預計蘋果將在 2026年佔據25%的市場份額，華為預計佔24%，摩托羅拉和榮耀緊隨其後，分別佔據8%和3%。（介面）

China 10Y Yield Returns to 1-Year Low — China’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 1.70% on Monday, returning to its lowest level since August 2025, as softer inflation data reinforced expectations that Beijing has greater flexibility to provide additional policy support in the remainder of the year. Annual consumer inflation eased to a six-month low of 0.5% in July, reflecting further declines in food prices and slower growth in non-food costs. Producer prices also slowed to 3.5% from 4.1%, marking its first deceleration since turning positive in March following the oil-price surge linked to tensions in the Middle East. At a recent meeting, the Political Bureau of the Communist Party of China Central Committee pledged more proactive and effective macroeconomic policies, faster deployment of fiscal funds and bond proceeds, and continued support for equipment upgrades and consumer goods trade-ins. It also stressed boosting domestic demand amid weak consumer spending, despite strong exports and industrial activity.

Soybeans Hold Near Multi-Week Lows — Soybean futures held around $11.5 per bushel, staying near five-week lows as traders adjusted positions ahead of the USDA’s upcoming crop report this week. The report will include the first survey-based estimate of 2026 US soybean yields and updated harvested acreage, potentially reshaping expectations for crop size and supplies. Meanwhile, weather has become less supportive, as warmer and drier conditions across parts of the Midwest raised concerns over soybean pod filling during August, adding uncertainty around US yields. While the USDA recently confirmed a private sale of 132,000 metric tons of US soybeans to China for delivery in the 2026/27 marketing year, the purchase did little to offset the bearish supply outlook. Traders now continued to monitor developments in the Black Sea region, where the ongoing Russia-Ukraine war threatened grain export routes, although expectations for another large harvest from the region continued to weigh on prices.

Indonesia Motorbike Sales Rise 8.3% n July — Motorbike sales in Indonesia grew 8.3% year-on-year to 636,030 units in July 2026, after a 1.1% rise in the previous month. The number was the largest since October 2014, amid easing price pressures in July. For the first seven months of the year, motorbike sales were 2.0% higher than in the same period last year, reaching 3.77 million units. The Association of Indonesian Motorcycle Industry (AISI) expects sales to remain relatively stable at around 6.4 million to 6.7 million units this year. Last year, motorbike sales climbed 1.3% to 6.4 million units, within the target range of 6.4 million to 6.7 million units set by AISI. However, the growth rate was slightly slower than the 1.5% increase recorded in 2024. On a monthly basis, motorbike sales surged 23.0% in July, accelerating sharply from 7.5% growth in June.

【美銀：下調申洲國際目標價至50港元，維持“買入”評級】申洲國際預期今年上半年純利同比下跌38%至43%，主要受到人民幣升值、原材料及勞工與退休金成本上升，以及銷量輕微下跌的拖累。美銀證券發表研究報告指，申洲國際中期盈利表現遜於市場預測的下跌30%至35%，相信主要是受到外匯因素的影響。該行目前預測申洲國際中期收入將下跌5%，毛利率料同比收窄逾3個百分點至24%，期內外匯虧損估算達到約6億元，預測政府補助因2025年每股盈利下跌而減少約1億元，綜合預測上半年純利將下跌40%。該行將申洲2026及2027年每股盈利預測分別下調12%及8%，以反映較高的外匯虧損及較低的毛利率，並將目標價從53.2港元下調至50港元，維持“買入”評級。

Indonesia Consumer Morale Drops to 15-Month Low — Indonesia’s consumer confidence index fell to 116.8 in July 2026 from 117.8 in the previous month, marking the lowest level since April 2025. The moderation was largely driven by weaker assessments of current economic conditions, which dropped by 1.3 points to 107.9. Sentiment toward durable goods purchases also declined by 1.8 points to 104.1, while perceptions of job availability over the past six months fell by 0.7 points to 101.1. Moreover, expectations for income over the next six months remained steady at 133.6. At the same time, expectations regarding job availability also fell by 1.3 points to 123.1, while expectations for business activity decreased by 0.8 points to 120.4. However, the index remained in optimistic territory, indicating that consumer confidence was still positive, supported by strong perceptions of current economic conditions and favorable expectations for the future.

### 2026-08-10 10:58:05  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Magnificent 7 #Star Stocks #Alphabet #Meta Platforms #GOOGL.O #Impact bearish #US10Y #Impact mixed #AMZN.O

【Academy Securities: AI capex creates equity–credit divergence; AI infrastructure bonds attractive】Academy Securities strategist Peter Tchir says AI capital spending is producing a rare equity–credit split. He remains cautious on AI equity valuations, warning markets could renew scrutiny of capex scale and returns. By contrast he favors bonds financing data centers and AI infrastructure for their higher total yields and potential for spread tightening. Alphabet’s recent plan to raise about $20–25 bln in the US bond market to fund large-scale AI investment exemplifies the dynamic; markets have already flagged capex-driven pressure on Alphabet’s free cash flow and equity valuation. Reuters data show Amazon, Meta, Oracle and other tech giants have also stepped up debt issuance this year. The same AI outlay can mean cash-burn and return-risk for shareholders while offering yield pickup to credit investors, prompting a shift from equity-only AI trades toward repricing within capital structures.

【中鎢線上：鎢精礦價格暫穩 鎢廢料價格上行】據中鎢線上公眾號訊息，8月10日，鎢價延續多空博弈下的僵持維穩態勢，市場交投活躍度受限。供應端普遍維持低價惜售態度，與此同時探高資源流通不暢。需求端則普遍呈現觀望姿態，採購行為集中於剛需匹配，市場成交增量有限。上週五，廣東某礦企公開競價銷售54標噸24.17%白鎢精礦，成交價39.3萬元/標噸。據中鎢線上資料，當前55%鎢精礦價格穩固在41萬元/標噸水平，仲鎢酸銨（APT）價格僵持在61萬元/噸左右，鎢粉與碳化鎢粉報價970元/公斤、920元/公斤，70鎢鐵價格在66萬元/噸左右盤整。鎢廢料價格試探性上行，但成交矛盾仍舊明顯。持貨商因高位持倉成本、上週原料長單定價與政策面相關訊息推動，挺市意願增強，報價有所上探。截至發稿，廢鎢棒材價格約770元/公斤，國產刀片價格約628元/公斤。但採購商態度明顯猶豫，接貨積極性相對減弱，市場總體成交放量受阻。業內人士指出，當前再生碳化鎢粉與原生碳化鎢粉的價格差距縮小，在一定程度上降低下游硬質合金企業對廢料的採購意願。

US 10-Year Yield Pressured by Soft Jobs Data — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note held around 4.66% on Monday but remained subdued after weaker-than-expected US employment data lowered expectations for a near-term Federal Reserve interest rate hike. Nonfarm payrolls unexpectedly declined by 23,000 in July, while sizeable downward revisions to the previous two months reinforced signs of a weakening labor market. 【寧德時代高能量密度航空電池透過相鄰電芯熱失控不擴散驗證】低空經濟迎來關鍵安全技術突破。記者從寧德時代獲悉，其為載人版eVTOL（電動垂直起降飛行器）研發的航空動力電池系統，成功透過相鄰電芯熱失控不擴散驗證。這是全球首個採用能量密度高達350Wh/kg的方殼電芯並順利透過該項測試的航空電池系統，標誌著高能量密度航空電池安全水平邁上新臺階。不同位置觸發，全程無熱擴散。此次驗證過程中，測試人員在電池系統中間、角落等不同位置，同時觸發相鄰兩顆電芯發生熱失控。結果顯示，系統成功實現無熱擴散，未引發連鎖反應。這一突破為低空經濟規模化發展提供了更堅實的安全動力保障，也是寧德時代持續探索動力電池安全邊界的又一實證。（福建日報）

Indonesia Shares Rise Hover at Over 2-Week Peak — Indonesian equities rose 47 points or 0.7% to 6,455 in early deals on Monday, extending last week’s momentum. Gains were broad-based across cyclicals, basic materials, energy, and financials, lifting the market to its highest level in more than two weeks. Sentiment was supported by stronger-than-expected Q2 GDP growth and a sizeable IDR 70 trillion liquidity injection into state-owned banks. Meanwhile, July’s forex reserves held steady around USD 145 billion, underscoring resilient external buffers. However, caution lingered after weak CPI and PPI readings from top trading partner China signaled fragile demand, even as easing oil shocks from the Middle East conflict tempered cost pressures. Among notable movers were Hartadinata Abadi (5.6%), Indosat (2.8%), Vale Indonesia (1.9%), and Perusahaan Gas Negara (1.0%). Traders now brace for Indonesia's July consumer sentiment after June's figures slipped to a nine-month low.

### 2026-08-10 10:23:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 0

South Africa Forex Reserves Fall to 8-Month Low — Gross foreign exchange reserves in South Africa fell to USD 73.45 billion in July 2026 from USD 74.11 billion in the previous month, marking the lowest level since November last year. The decline was mainly driven by lower foreign currency reserves, which fell to USD 50.41 billion from USD 51.22 billion. In contrast, gold reserves increased to USD 16.38 billion from USD 16.26 billion, while SDR holdings rose to USD 6.67 billion from USD 6.63 billion. The forward position increased to USD 1.19 billion from USD 0.59 billion, while foreign currency deposits received by the central bank declined to USD 2.88 billion from USD 3.36 billion. The South African Reserve Bank said the changes reflected a USD 150 million foreign loan from the OPEC Fund, higher US dollar gold prices and valuation adjustments, partly offset by government foreign exchange payments, including repayment of a USD 574 million foreign loan.

Yen Remains Under Pressure — The Japanese yen weakened past 158 per dollar on Monday, reversing gains from the previous session as a recent joint intervention by Tokyo and Washington failed to sustain the rally amid persistent structural pressure on the currency. The yen remained weighed down by wide interest rate differentials, mounting fiscal concerns and elevated energy and import costs. Latest data also showed Japan’s current account surplus narrowed in June, as strong exports of AI-related electronics were offset by higher imports driven by increased crude oil purchases. Meanwhile, the Bank of Japan highlighted growing risks of accelerating inflation in its summary of opinions from the July meeting, with one board member suggesting the pace of interest rate hikes could quicken. On Friday, the yen strengthened sharply against the dollar after weaker-than-expected US jobs data prompted traders to reduce expectations for a near-term Federal Reserve interest rate hike.

Taiwan Stocks Rise to Near 4-Week High — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, advanced 933 points, or 2.1%, to 45,159 in Monday morning trade, erasing losses from the previous two sessions and tracking a rally on Wall Street on Friday after weaker-than-expected jobs data eased concerns over Fed rate hikes at the upcoming meeting. The broader index hit its highest level since July 16 after consolidating in the previous session amid concerns over massive spending on AI. The increase was mainly driven by electronic technology stocks, which rose 2.7%. TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market's total value, climbed 1.1%, while MediaTek rose 1.3%, and Delta Electronics surged 10.0%. However, rising oil prices limited the gains, as concerns over inflation raised expectations of interest rate hikes. Traders also anticipated the release of US inflation data due this week for clues about the Fed's monetary policy decision at its upcoming meeting.

【Westpac: Downside risks realised, CPI cools; RBA likely to hold in August】Westpac expects the RBA to keep the cash rate at 4.35% in August after Q2 headline and trimmed-mean CPI printed below forecasts, a call consistent with Commonwealth Bank of Australia. Both banks note pass-through from Middle East-related energy costs to consumer prices has not persisted in recent months; June inflation showed higher input costs did not broadly reach consumers. ### 2026-08-10 10:15:00  #公司

【小紅書再陷期權爭議：前員工稱期權歸屬僅剩8天，遭遇公司“踩點辭退”】8月10日訊息，近日，有媒體訊息稱，繼原小紅書商業化華南直銷負責人陳浩公開期權維權經歷後，又一名前小紅書員工姜東透過公開平臺發文，披露其與小紅書之間一場持續多年的期權訴訟爭議。

據姜東講述，他於2018年9月入職書行科技（北京）有限公司（小紅書旗下公司），擔任首席技術專家，後擔任媒體智慧團隊負責人。姜東稱，自己入職時獲承諾授予80萬股境外公司期權，約定工作滿2年歸屬50%。在職期間，姜東於2020年1月被額外授予6萬股期權。但7個月後，小紅書突然解除了與姜東的勞動關係。按姜東說法，此時距離他入職滿兩年、首批50%期權歸屬僅剩8天。

此後，姜東與小紅書圍繞解約及期權歸屬問題進入司法程式，姜東要求小紅書兌其期權權益。判決書顯示，小紅書一方辯稱，（姜東）第一次行權需在授予日滿2年，即2020年10月30日，但（姜東）的勞動關係已於2020年8月25日合法解除。此外，小紅書還提出與姜東解除勞動關係是因其曠工等。最終，法院認定公司解除勞動關係合法，駁回姜東全部訴訟請求。

姜東則認為，小紅書解除員工勞動關係的時間節點是“經過精心算計”。據姜東講述，大量同事都在關鍵行權節點前，被公司以不同理由辭退，他所在的同事群中，有幾十人都是這種情況。

對此情況，新浪科技向小紅書官方進行求證，截至發稿暫無回應。（新浪科技）

Shanghai Stocks Hit Near 1-Month High — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.6% to a near one-month high of 3,965 on Monday, while the Shenzhen Component slipped 0.5% to 14,233, as investors assessed soft inflation data that reinforced expectations of policy support for the economy. Annual consumer inflation eased to a six-month low of 0.5% in July, driven by further declines in food prices and slower growth in non-food costs. Producer price inflation also moderated to 3.5% from 4.1%, marking its first slowdown since turning positive in March following the oil-price surge linked to the Iran conflict. China's leaders vowed to bolster economic growth through faster infrastructure spending, seeking to offset persistent weakness in domestic demand despite resilient exports and factory activity. PetroChina (1.4%), Kweichow Moutai (1.4%), and Foxconn Industrial Internet (2.6%) were among the top gainers. New Zealand Dollar Holds Firm — The New Zealand dollar traded around $0.588 on Monday after rising to a more than two-month high in the previous session as a weak US jobs report sent the greenback lower. 【Sony, TSMC plan ¥1 trillion image-sensor JV in Kumamoto, target 2029 mass production】Sony Group and TSMC (TSM.N) plan a joint venture to mass-produce next-generation image sensors in Kumamoto, southwestern Japan, targeting production as early as 2029. Sony would hold about 60% and TSMC about 40%; total investment is expected around ¥1 trillion (about $6.3 bln). The partners expect to finalise large-scale production investment soon and form the JV by the end of fiscal 2026 (March 2027). The plant is intended to supply high-performance camera sensors for Apple iPhones and to improve sensor performance for AI object recognition aimed at emerging "physical AI" applications.

### 2026-08-10 10:10:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #Hong Kong #Stock Market #Importance 1

Hong Kong Stocks Begin Week Higher — The Hang Seng Index rose 0.8%, or 200 points, to 25,873 on Monday, tracking broader Asian gains after softer-than-expected US jobs data reduced expectations of an imminent Federal Reserve rate hike. Lower Treasury yields and a weaker US dollar boosted risk appetite, particularly for technology and growth stocks. Sentiment also improved after China's annual inflation eased to 0.5% in July from 1.0% in June, the lowest reading in six months. Investors also watched Guotai Junan International ahead of its trading resumption following its parent's HK$28.6 billion take-private proposal, signaling further consolidation in China's brokerage sector. Hong Kong's longer-term appeal remained supported by HKEX's role as a gateway for international capital, though geopolitical tensions and elevated oil prices continued to pose risks. Notable movers included Tencent (0.7%), AIA (0.8%), Wuxi Biologics (4.5%), Xiaomi (0.4%) and Genscript Biotech (0.4%).

【兩部門聯合啟動2026年度計量支撐產業新質生產力發展專案徵集工作】記者今天（10日）瞭解到，市場監管總局、工業和資訊化部近日聯合印發通知，公開徵集2026年度計量支撐產業新質生產力發展專案。此舉旨在深入貫徹落實《計量支撐產業新質生產力發展行動方案（2025—2030年）》部署要求，圍繞戰略性新興產業和未來產業新質生產力發展迫切需求，持續發揮計量在科技創新、產業升級中的基礎性、戰略性作用，加快破解產業發展關鍵計量技術瓶頸，推動產業高質量發展。本次專案徵集圍繞新一代資訊科技、人工智慧、航空航天、新能源、新材料、高階裝備、生物醫藥、量子科技、積體電路、儀器儀表十大重點領域，聚焦產業鏈關鍵共性計量裝備研製、計量技術攻關與產業化應用，重點支援能夠破解“卡脖子”難題、實現國產替代、填補國內外空白的重大創新專案。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-10 09:38:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Rise on Tech Gains — The benchmark KOSPI edged up 0.5% to around 6,290 on Monday, rebounding from the previous session as gains in technology stocks tracked a record-setting advance on Wall Street. The S&P 500 climbed to a fresh record while the Nasdaq advanced on Friday after a weaker-than-expected US jobs report showed an unexpected contraction in July employment, easing expectations of a September Federal Reserve rate hike. The improved rate outlook supported demand for technology stocks, with Samsung Electronics and SK hynix both rising around 1%. Other gainers included Hyundai Motor (+2.8%), Hanwha Aerospace (+4.4%), HD Hyundai Heavy Industries (+2.0%), and Doosan Enerbility (+2.1%). Meanwhile, uncertainty over a US-Iran deal to reopen the Strait of Hormuz persisted after Iran denied direct talks with Washington, tempering the broader recovery in the KOSPI. WTI crude also rose above $79 a barrel, raising concerns over higher energy costs and inflation.

### 2026-08-10 09:38:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Interest Rate #Importance 1

BoJ Sees Rising Case for Further Tightening: July Summary of Opinions — Bank of Japan’s July summary of opinions revealed policymakers see room to keep raising rates as underlying inflation nears 2% and financial conditions remain supportive. They stressed the need to judge timing and pace carefully, watching economic activity, prices, financial conditions, and external factors such as Middle East tensions, AI-driven demand, and currency moves. Several noted rising upside risks to inflation, with one view suggesting hikes could come faster than markets anticipate if conditions warrant. The summary underscored that the BoJ has entered a new phase requiring flexibility rather than a preset path, as concerns over weak growth have eased and inflationary pressures may strengthen into summer. Policymakers also emphasized the importance of clearly signaling determination to prevent excessive price gains, highlighting a shift toward more nimble, risk-aware policy management.

Gasoline Gains for Third Session — US gasoline futures rose above $3 per gallon on Monday, gaining for a third consecutive session, as uncertainty persisted over the reopening of the Strait of Hormuz. Iran said an agreement with Oman on new shipping routes was close to being finalized but warned that reopening the waterway remained conditional on Washington meeting additional demands. Tehran also ruled out direct talks with the US for now, saying it would not engage while Washington continues to violate an interim agreement reached in June, despite US assertions that a deal is close. Oil supply risks were further underscored by claims from Iran-aligned Houthis that they had struck Saudi Aramco’s Jazan refinery. Elsewhere, escalating attacks between Russia and Ukraine have increased the threat of further strikes on energy facilities, following Ukraine’s recent long-range drone attack on a major Russian refinery. Moscow has since extended its ban on gasoline and diesel exports through January 2027.

【交通運輸部檢查指導組在廣西檢查時發現，部分企業安全生產主體責任落實存在短板】交通運輸部檢查指導組赴廣西壯族自治區南寧、貴港、來賓、玉林、柳州、欽州、北海等7個地級市22個縣（市、區），開展汛期暑期交通運輸安全生產重大風險隱患排查整治指導工作，共發現問題隱患93項，其中突出問題4項。檢查指導組在博白縣致遠運輸有限公司檢查時發現，動態監控平臺顯示，該企業普貨運輸車輛在2026年1月1日至6月24日期間存在33起超速行為。其中，2026年2月23日該車輛因超速被公安交管部門處罰，但運輸過程中企業未及時提醒糾正、運輸行為結束後一個月內未嚴肅處理。此外，2025年以來該車輛被公安交管部門處罰13起，其中超速2起。檢查指導組還發現，359國道、304省道存在邊溝堵塞無法排水等問題。

Heating Oil Rises for 4th Session — US heating oil futures rose to around $3.96 per gallon on Monday, gaining for a fourth consecutive session, driven by uncertainty over the reopening of the Strait of Hormuz. Iran said a deal with Oman to establish new shipping lanes was nearing completion but stressed that the waterway would only reopen if Washington met additional conditions. Tehran also said it was not engaged in direct negotiations with the US and would not initiate talks while Washington continues to breach an interim deal signed in June, despite US claims that an agreement is near. Supply concerns were further heightened after Iran-aligned Houthis said they attacked Saudi Aramco’s Jazan refinery. Meanwhile, Russia and Ukraine have stepped up attacks on each other, raising the risk of further strikes on energy facilities after Ukraine recently carried out a long-range drone attack on a major Russian oil refinery. Against this backdrop, Moscow extended its gasoline and diesel export ban through January 2027.

### 2026-08-10 09:28:51  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Rises Amid Uncertainty Over Hormuz — European natural gas prices rose above €56 per MWh on Monday, recouping part of the losses from the previous week amid uncertainty over the normalization of shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. Iran and Oman remained short of a final agreement over the weekend after Tehran renewed a lengthy list of demands for Washington as conditions for a full reopening of the strategic waterway, indicating any immediate relief for gas supplies could remain limited. Although Iran indicated that a deal was close, it cautioned that this did not mean the strait would reopen imminently and continued to reject direct negotiations with the US. With a deal still elusive, disruptions to LNG shipments through the key waterway are likely to persist, delaying cargoes from major exporters such as Qatar. The ongoing delays have kept global supply balances tight, intensifying competition with Asian buyers for available cargoes and hampering Europe's winter stockpiling efforts.

【長江防總、長江委啟動Ⅳ級應急響應】8月7日，長江委組織會商，針對性安排部署颱風暴雨洪水防禦工作。會商指出，受颱風“白海豚”影響，8月9日至12日，長江中下游幹流及兩湖水系有一次強降雨過程。預計長江下游幹流及附近青弋水陽江、滁河、巢湖水系等支流，鄱陽湖五河、洞庭湖湘江上游和陸水等長江中游幹流附近支流將發生漲水過程，區域性強降雨引發山洪災害的風險高，部分中小河流可能發生超警及以上洪水。長江防總、長江委於8月7日21時對江西、安徽、江蘇、上海4省（市）分別啟動防汛Ⅳ級應急響應和洪水防禦Ⅳ級應急響應，並向湖北、湖南、江西、安徽、江蘇、上海6省（市）釋出汛情通報，提醒近期颱風強降雨過程，要求針對性做好颱風暴雨洪水防禦工作，全力保障人民群眾生命財產安全。長江委已預置2個工作組，根據颱風“白海豚”防禦需要，隨時趕赴一線做好技術支撐。

Australia Stocks Ease Further as New Week Opens — Australian shares fell 45 points, or 0.5%, to 9,218 in early trading on Monday, extending losses for a second session as U.S. stock futures weakened amid growing doubts that the U.S. and Iran will soon reach a deal to reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Caution also lingered ahead of the Reserve Bank’s policy decision later this week, as sticky costs cloud the outlook despite three hikes so far this year. Data from main trading partner China added to the unease, with July inflation easing to a six-month low and producer prices rising the least in three months, underscoring soft demand. Financials led declines: the big four banks fell between 1.4% and 4.6% after Westpac’s soft Q3 update. Insurers followed lower, with QBE (-2.3%), Insurance Australia (-2.5%) and AUB Group (-1.7%) among the laggards. Losses also spread to consumer durables, commercial services and logistics. Offsetting the weakness, miners advanced, with BHP (1.1%), Northern Star (1.8%) and Evolution (1.5%) posting solid gains.

### 2026-08-10 09:18:50  #資料 #市場 #央行 #國際

【日本央行會議紀要提及物價上行風險 加息步伐可能加快】日本央行在7月會議紀要摘要中指出，通脹上行風險正在上升，其中一名委員提到加息步伐可能會加快。根據週一釋出的7月30日至31日會議紀要摘要，九名理事會成員中的一位表示：“鑑於核心CPI通脹率一直接近2%，且應比以往更加重視物價的上行風險，因此可以認為政策利率的加息步伐將快於市場預期。”在此次會議上，日本央行將政策利率維持在1%，並暗示可能在9月加息，稱正密切關注日元貶值對物價和經濟增長的影響。這一決定恰逢日本當局在美國同行協助下介入外匯市場以支撐日元之後。上月，日元兌美元匯率跌至40年低點，引發了市場對日本進口通脹以及全球金融市場穩定面臨風險的擔憂。九名董事會成員中，僅Hajime Takata一人在上月呼籲連續加息。儘管如此，行長植田和男在決議後的新聞釋出會上仍採取了總體鷹派立場，強調他認為物價前景存在更大的上行風險。該會議紀要並未披露與會者各自的具體發言內容。一位委員在紀要中表示，隨著日本央行考慮進一步加息的時機和步伐，需要仔細評估中東局勢、人工智慧相關需求以及匯率走勢等因素的影響。“在此過程中，當前階段需要對物價基本趨勢的上行風險給予應有的關注，”該委員表示。一位日本央行委員表示，隨著全球環境進入加息階段，日本央行必須展現出防止通脹偏離上行的決心。該成員稱，日本央行“需要採取靈活的方式應對海外金融狀況變化等因素，並就加息幅度進行討論，而不是拘泥於某種加息節奏”。

【俄軍基地有新定位 俄敘關係開“新篇章”？】據敘利亞官方通訊社當地時間8月9日報道，敘過渡政府外交部當天稱，敘利亞與俄羅斯已達成一份諒解備忘錄，旨在重新調整俄方在敘海岸的軍事存在，包括將俄方基地的軍事設施轉變為聯合訓練中心，將民用性質設施移交給敘過渡政府管理。相關調整工作將在3個月內完成。敘方表示，該備忘錄是經過約一年半的密集談判後達成的，解決了俄羅斯在赫梅米姆和塔爾圖斯兩處基地的未來管理問題。自2024年年底敘利亞國內形勢劇變之後，目前俄羅斯在敘保留的軍事存在只剩西部地中海沿岸的塔爾圖斯海軍基地和赫梅米姆空軍基地。這兩個基地是俄羅斯全球軍事佈局的重要支點，可為俄軍往返地中海提供保障。（央視）

【胡塞武裝襲擊葉門政府控制港口】葉門胡塞武裝8月9日發表宣告稱，為回應“沙特支援的武裝力量”在葉門西海岸和塔伊茲省持續發動襲擊，胡塞武裝當天以塔伊茲省紅海沿岸摩卡港“沙特支援的武裝力量”為目標，動用大量彈道導彈和無人機，對其集結點和武器庫發動了“大規模、高強度”襲擊。宣告稱，襲擊造成相關武器裝備“大範圍損毀”，並“導致數十人死傷，其中包括沙特方面人員”。 葉門政府方面稱，襲擊造成多人死亡、數十人受傷，港口建築和碼頭嚴重損毀，商業貨物和食品供應受影響。 據瞭解，摩卡港是葉門政府控制的主要港口之一，經過翻新後，該港口已能夠處理避開胡塞武裝控制的荷臺達港的航運業務。 （CCTV國際時訊）

Dollar Holds Decline on Soft Jobs Data — The dollar index hovered around 99.6 on Monday after sliding sharply in the previous session, pressured by weaker-than-expected US employment data that lowered expectations for a near-term Federal Reserve interest rate hike. Nonfarm payrolls unexpectedly declined by 23,000 in July, while steep downward revisions to the prior two months further signaled a deterioration in labor market conditions. Markets now see around a 44% probability of a 25 basis point rate hike in September, down from 67% a week earlier. Investors are now turning their attention to key inflation data due this week for additional clues on the monetary policy outlook. Meanwhile, markets continued to track developments in the Middle East as Iran denied holding direct talks with the US, despite Washington’s claims that a deal was imminent. Tehran also maintained demands for an end to the US naval blockade, the lifting of sanctions and compensation for war-related damage before agreeing to any deal.

【韓國散戶二季度轉為海外股票淨賣出 6月重啟加倉美股】今年第二季度，“西學螞蟻”（韓國散戶海外美股投資者）等個人投資者的海外股票投資時隔10個季度轉為淨賣出。分析認為，受韓國本土股市大幅上漲、韓元兌美元匯率飆升至1500韓元關口影響，不少資金一度捨棄美股，轉向韓國國內股市。 不過這場“迴歸本土股市”的行情並未持續太久。從6月開始，散戶再度恢復美股淨買入，進入7月後買入規模大幅擴張，西學螞蟻的資金風向重新轉回海外市場。 韓國央行經濟統計系統8月10日資料顯示，今年二季度，包含個人投資者在內的非金融企業海外股票投資錄得5.257億美元淨賣出。這是非金融企業海外股票投資自2023年第四季度（淨賣出14.726億美元）以來首次出現淨賣出。

### 2026-08-10 08:58:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Wheat Futures Rise as Supply Concerns Persist — Wheat prices rose to around $6.40 per bushel, remaining above a four-week low reached on August 6, as concerns over tightening global supplies outweighed improved crop prospects in Australia. Grain exports from the Black Sea region have been disrupted by intensified attacks on port infrastructure linked to the Russia–Ukraine war, while severe heatwaves across the US, Canada, and Europe have heightened concerns over crop yields and quality. In France, extreme temperatures are expected to reduce wheat production, while Canadian wheat acreage has declined from a year earlier. Meanwhile, timely rainfall across key growing regions in New South Wales, Queensland, and Victoria has boosted yield expectations, prompting Rabobank to raise its production forecast to as much as 30 million tons. Bendigo Bank Agribusiness has also upgraded its outlook to around 30 million tons, with output potentially reaching 33 million tons.

AUS 10Y Yield Holds RBA Decision Looms — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield held above 4.9%, moving near multi-week highs as investors looked ahead to the Reserve Bank of Australia’s monetary policy decision on Tuesday. The central bank is widely expected to keep its cash rate unchanged at 4.35% for a second straight meeting, although policymakers are expected to maintain a hawkish stance as inflation remains elevated. Markets are pricing around a 50% probability of another 25-basis-point rate hike by year-end, while traders will closely watch the RBA’s updated forecasts and Governor Michele Bullock’s comments for clues on the future policy path. Elsewhere, persistent uncertainty over efforts to reopen the Strait of Hormuz drove oil prices higher and fueled concerns over elevated inflation. Iran said talks with Oman on a shipping route through the strait were nearing an agreement, but stressed that the waterway would not reopen immediately., while Tehran also rejected direct talks with the US for now.

【美國7月超150個城鎮和縣出臺資料中心禁令】今年夏季，美國地方政府抵制資料中心建設的趨勢持續加劇，這對Anthropic、谷歌、OpenAI等大型人工智慧企業而言是預警訊號——這些公司正依靠擴建資料中心實現算力擴張目標。 僅7月一個月，就有超過150個城鎮和縣出臺資料中心臨時或永久禁令，不少禁令透過緊急會議倉促表決落地。分析顯示：截至8月初，全美現行有效的資料中心禁令數量已突破500項。6月末首次上線禁令追蹤統計時，統計數量僅300餘項。 美國各縣、城鎮叫停相關專案，源於當地居民多重顧慮：資料中心推高居民電費、消耗稀缺水資源、產生大量噪音。專案支援方則反駁，這類工程可為鄉村、工業區創造就業、吸引投資、推動基礎設施升級，居民部分擔憂存在誇大。但相關觀點未能遏制禁令出臺的浪潮。

Silver Holds Gain After Weak US Jobs Report — Silver traded near $64 an ounce on Monday after surging more than 10% last week, supported by an unexpected contraction in the US labor market that led traders to reduce expectations for a near-term Federal Reserve interest rate hike. Data released Friday showed the US economy unexpectedly lost 23K jobs in July, following a downwardly revised 20K increase in June and falling well short of forecasts for an 80K gain. Markets now price around a 44% chance of a 25 basis point rate hike in September, down from 67% a week earlier. Silver also held its gains even after oil prices climbed higher as uncertainty persisted over efforts to reopen the Strait of Hormuz. Iran said talks with Oman were close to an agreement but denied holding direct negotiations with the US, despite Washington’s claims that a deal was imminent.

### 2026-08-10 08:12:52  #公司

【康諾亞-B：CM512(TSLP x IL-13雙抗)注射液用於慢性鼻竇炎伴鼻息肉患者的II期臨床試驗達到所有研究終點】康諾亞-B(02162)釋出公告，公司自主研發的1類新藥CM512注射液治療慢性鼻竇炎伴鼻息肉(CRSwNP)的II期臨床研究已完成資料揭盲及初步統計分析。結果顯示：1、CM512達到了全部研究終點，各劑量組療效表現相當。CM512快速起效，給藥第4周即可快速縮小鼻息肉同時顯著改善鼻塞，並促進嗅覺功能恢復。給藥一針療效可維持半年，24周鼻息肉評分(NPS)較基線變化顯著優於對照組，同時明顯減輕整體鼻竇腔炎症。2、在CRSwNP患者中，CM512所有劑量組均展現出良好的安全性和耐受性，抗藥抗體(ADA)的發生率極低。治療期間不良事件(TEAEs)發生率與安慰劑組相當，CM512組未發生嚴重不良事件。詳細資料未來將於國際學術期刊或學術會議進一步公佈。基於此II期結果，公司正在快速啟動CM512注射液治療CRSwNP的臨床III期研究，並明確300mg，半年給藥一次(Q24W)為用藥方案。

Japan Current Account Surplus Below Estimates — Japan’s current account surplus decreased to JPY 923.0 billion in June 2026 from JPY 1,281.8 billion in the same month a year earlier, missing market expectations for a JPY 1,512 billion surplus. The goods account swung to a JPY 135.2 billion deficit from a JPY 467.3 billion surplus, as exports rose 16.3%, trailing a 24.3% increase in imports. Meanwhile, the services deficit widened to JPY 228.5 billion from JPY 160.6 billion. The primary income surplus also fell sharply to JPY 380.1 billion from JPY 1,444.9 billion. In contrast, the secondary income deficit narrowed to JPY 108.6 billion from JPY 469.8 billion.

【澳大利亞富邑葡萄酒集團新增近3.95億美元資產減值，或考慮退出美國市場】澳大利亞生產商富邑葡萄酒集團(Treasury Wine Estates)可能會考慮退出美國市場。此前該公司宣佈，為應對全球消費者需求下降的局面，將進行近3.95億美元的新增資產減值。富邑葡萄酒集團表示，將繼續對其在美國的業務進行戰略評估。該公司在2021年至2023年間曾斥資13億美元大舉收購，積累了加州資產，並已開始對這些資產進行精簡。這家在澳大利亞證券交易所(ASX)上市的生產商周一表示：“對美洲業務的運營和戰略評估仍在進行中，富邑葡萄酒集團已任命顧問，以支援對美洲品牌組合、運營模式和資產基礎的所有可用選項進行評估。”富邑葡萄酒集團表示，將立即削減產量，並透過大宗渠道處理多餘的美國庫存。該公司此前已預告，計劃出售部分加州釀酒廠並整合生產，以降低成本。這一最新舉措將產生5.584億澳元（合3.947億美元）的非現金成本。此前，富邑葡萄酒集團已有一筆7.705億澳元的資產減值，該減值導致公司在截至12月的半個財年錄得6.494億澳元的法定虧損。

### 2026-08-10 08:02:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Loan Growth #Importance 1

Japan Bank Lending Growth Eases in July — Japan’s bank lending rose 5.4% year-on-year in July 2026, slowing from a five-year high of 5.7% increase in the previous month. Outstanding loans at major, regional, and shinkin banks averaged JPY 679.2 trillion, up from JPY 679.0 trillion in June. Lending at major and regional banks increased 5.9%, easing from 6.2% in June, with major-bank lending growth slowing to 8.0% from 8.6%, while regional-bank lending remained unchanged at 4.2%. Within regional banks, lending growth was steady at 4.6% for regional banks I and 2.4% for regional banks II. Meanwhile, lending by shinkin banks accelerated to 1.9% from 1.7%, marking the strongest growth since early 2023.

【我科研團隊破解超導量子計算“速度與保真度”矛盾】記者9日從安徽省量子計算晶片重點實驗室獲悉，由本源量子與中國科學技術大學聯合研究團隊創新性提出的“引數空間擴充套件受控相位門（PSE-CZ）”方案，成功破解了超導量子計算領域長期存在的速度與保真度相互制約難題，讓兩位元量子門在保持高速的同時大幅提升精度。相關成果發表於國際物理學術期刊《物理評論快報》，實驗驗證工作在我國自主超導量子計算機“本源悟空”上完成。（科技日報）

【公安部網安局再次公佈15起涉汛涉災網路謠言案例】公安部網安局在前期2次集中公佈涉汛涉災網路謠言典型案例的基礎上，8月10日再次公佈15起相關案例。這次公佈的案例涉及廣東、安徽、甘肅等地，個別網民為博取關注、吸粉引流，仍在惡意編造涉汛涉災虛假內容，有的甚至使用AI工具篡改當地政府釋出的安全通知，嚴重干擾防汛救災工作，造成不良影響。公安機關網安部門迅速行動、嚴厲打擊。目前，所有案涉屬地公安機關均已對相關涉事人員依法行政處罰。公安部網安局表示將持續嚴厲打擊編造涉汛涉災網路謠言違法犯罪活動，同時提示公眾，根據治安管理處罰法規定，故意散佈謠言，謊報險情、疫情、災情、警情或者以其他方法故意擾亂公共秩序的，處十日以下拘留，可以並處罰款；造成嚴重後果的，將以編造、故意傳播虛假資訊罪追究刑事責任。（新華社）

【美參議院加密監管法案投票推遲至9月 《清晰法案》落地前景不容樂觀】此前，美國參議院進入八月休會期，並未就加密貨幣監管法案舉行投票，該法案在中期選舉前獲得透過的可能性顯著下降。 加密行業仍寄望於《透明度法案》（Clarity Act）能夠在議員9月重返國會、舉行為期三週的短期會議期間在參議院闖關。該法案旨在為加密行業搭建一套監管框架。 但國會議員下月復會後待辦議程繁重；且參議院共和黨人與支援加密行業的民主黨人尚未就一項倫理條款達成共識，這一條款仍是談判的核心阻礙。 資本阿爾法諮詢董事合夥人Ian Katz接受採訪時表示：“我認為9月透過的機率會下降。審議視窗期很短，可用立法工作日不多，各類議題都會爭奪議程空間。”

【經濟日報：中國開源AI，正在惠及全球】日前，MiniMax正式開源H3模型。短短三天，它便衝上全球最大AI開源平臺Hugging Face熱度榜首，超百家企業實現“Day 0接入”，從晶片廠商到推理框架完成全鏈路適配。更耐人尋味的是，開源領域的標誌性人物、Stable Diffusion模型創始人埃馬德·莫斯塔克也罕見發聲，公開“向MiniMax致敬”。對全球開發者而言，中國開源模型提供了成本更低、價效比更高的選擇，實實在在拉低了人工智慧落地的門檻。 資料可以佐證這股助力：有美國投資機構統計，多達八成的美國AI初創公司在融資路演中會使用中國的開源模型；MiniMax服務的全球企業與開發者客戶已超百萬，較半年前增長五倍。放眼全球，中國研發的開源模型下載量已佔全球總量的41%，躍居世界第一；在全球主流大模型呼叫榜單上，排名前六的模型全部來自中國團隊。展望未來，全球AI生態仍將沿著開閉源兩條路徑快速演進。而中國AI大模型在這一程序中展現出的開放姿態與生態引力，無疑會讓它在全球創新版圖中扮演更加重要的角色。

### 2026-08-10 07:49:11  #其他

【我國發現稀散金屬獨立新礦物——烏斯河鍺礦】中國科學院廣州地球化學研究所訊息，近日，由長安大學、中國科學院廣州地球化學研究所、中國科學院地球化學研究所等單位科研團隊聯合發現、命名並申報的新礦物——烏斯河鍺礦（Wusiheite，編號：IMA2025-083a，礦物縮寫：Wsh），於2026年8月正式獲得國際礦物學協會新礦物命名及分類委員會批准。烏斯河鍺礦是我國發現的首個獨立鍺酸鹽礦物，其氧化鍺的平均含量高達72%，僅次於鍺石（GeO2），也是繼瑞忠鍺礦、文慶鍺礦後，在該礦區發現的第3個富鍺礦物。該發現不僅明確了烏斯河鉛鋅礦床中關鍵的富鍺載體礦物，也為揭示富鍺熱液流體演化以及稀散金屬鍺的超常富集機制提供了至關重要的礦物學直接證據。

【China's newly discovered germanium mineral approved by IMA】Chang'an University said a new mineral discovered and named jointly with the Guangzhou Institute of Geochemistry, Chinese Academy of Sciences, and other institutes — the Wushe River germanium mineral — received approval from the International Mineralogical Association's Commission on New Minerals, Nomenclature and Classification in August. It is the first independently occurring germanate mineral discovered in China. Germanium is a strategic metal used in semiconductors, fiber‑optic communications, infrared optics and solar PV; it has low crustal abundance, rarely forms independent ore bodies, and has typically been recovered as an associated element in lead‑zinc ores and coal.

【年化收益超15% 12只長跑基金圍繞三大核心投資邏輯踏準節奏】經歷7月大幅回撥後，8月科技板塊迎來強勢反彈，持有相關產品的基金淨值快速修復。市場情緒隨之出現明顯分化：部分基民迎來回血鬆一口氣，也有投資者糾結是否借反彈視窗落袋為安。而這一心態分歧，恰恰暴露了投資科技主題基金的普遍困境——上漲階段難以堅定持有，踏空後又容易高位追入，反覆操作不斷損耗實際投資收益。怎樣的基金產品能跨越牛熊，在多輪科技行情中持續斬獲超額收益？證券時報基金研究院設定多重篩選條件：基金經理需在2019年6月前上任，同時在2019—2021年新能源、半導體行情，以及2024年至2026年8月7日的AI行情中，均跑贏萬得偏股混合型基金指數；幾何平均年化收益率超15%，任期最大回撤控制在45%以內，且2025年四季報、2026年二季報科技板塊持倉佔比均高於25%。依照上述標準，共篩選出12只主動權益基金，對應7位基金經理，來自易方達基金、富國基金、萬家基金、平安基金、東吳基金等5家基金公司。覆盤這12只產品跨越兩輪科技浪潮的運作路徑，可總結出三大核心投資邏輯：精準行業輪動、前瞻產業調研、嚴格估值紀律。

### 2026-08-10 07:15:28  #市場

【回血速度驚人 近160只基金短期反彈超20%】回撥了一個月的科技板塊，終於在上週企穩回升，迎來振奮人心的“四連陽”。8月7日，PCB、儲存晶片等科技細分賽道強勢反彈，萬得光通訊概念指數、國證儲存晶片產業指數分別大漲3.21%、4.12%，如果拉長時間來看，兩隻指數分別在近四個交易日反彈了約20%。隨著科技主線的強勢歸來，不少重倉科技股的公募基金也順勢迎來了暴漲行情，短期淨值回血速度驚人。Wind資料顯示，近四個交易日，近160只基金上漲超20%，其中兩隻基金的漲幅甚至超過了30%。在科技反彈帶來的淨值飆升狂歡下，也必須清醒地認識到，控制回撤往往比追求單次反彈的高度更為重要。可以簡單算一筆賬：那些在7月份回撤了40%的高彈性科技基金，如果想要重回前期高點，淨值需要累計上漲66.7%。雖然它們在最近4個交易日裡迅猛反彈了30%，但距離真正回本和創新高，依然有著不小的距離。反觀在7月份透過主動避險，將回撤控制在12%左右的易方達供給改革，想要重回高點僅僅需要上漲13.6%。因此，即使它的反彈彈性沒有那些超跌科技基金那麼大，卻能以極高的效率收復失地。

### 2026-08-10 07:10:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Industrial Production #Importance 0

Argentina Industrial Output Rebounds in June — Argentina’s industrial production grew by 2% year-on-year in June 2026, recovering from a downwardly revised 5.6% drop in the previous month. Ten of the 16 manufacturing sectors posted annual increases. The strongest gains came from basic metals (11.8%), chemicals and chemical products (11.0%), other transport equipment (7.2%), food and beverages (6.2%), wood, paper and printing (8.4%), metal products (3.6%), petroleum refining (3.4%), furniture and other manufacturing (3.4%), and tobacco products (0.4%). Meanwhile, output declined in other equipment, appliances and instruments (-25.3%), machinery and equipment (-16.8%), textiles (-15.9%), apparel, leather and footwear (-8.4%), non-metallic mineral products (-7.2%), motor vehicles and auto parts (-3.5%), and rubber and plastic products (-2.9%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production rose 0.9%, accelerating from an downwardly revised 0.3% gain in May.

### 2026-08-10 07:08:06  #公司

【人民日報經濟時評：從上汽集團與通用汽車續約談起】上汽集團與通用汽車近日續約20年，通用汽車成為又一家選擇“長期繫結”中國市場的跨國車企。此前，廣汽集團與本田汽車也已簽署合資續約協議。在銷量下滑、市場份額萎縮的背景下，續約的原因值得思考。 資料顯示，廣汽本田年銷量從超80萬輛降至約35萬輛，降幅達56%；上汽通用在華銷量從200多萬輛降至2025年的53.5萬輛，流失近3/4。數字雖然冰冷，但跨國車企的選擇卻是明確的——堅決留在中國市場。 原因顯而易見：今天的中國，已不僅是汽車領域“全球最大市場”，更是“最強考場”。中國消費者對智慧座艙的配置規格要求嚴苛，對智慧輔助駕駛的期待更為高漲，對新能源轉型的接受意願強。技術快速迭代、越級配置、好而不貴……這些僅僅是一款中國暢銷車型的必備要素。跨國車企那套一款車型包打天下的“全球車”邏輯，在中國市場越來越行不通。

NZX 50 Edges Higher to Start the Week — New Zealand's stocks climbed 34 points, or 0.2%, to 13,858 in Monday morning trade, halting losses in the previous two sessions, tracking a rise on Wall Street on Friday after softer-than-expected labor data dampened expectations of a Fed rate hike at the September meeting. Positive trade data from China, New Zealand's top trading partner, with export growth remaining robust, continued to help cap the losses, while inflation eased to a six-month low. However, traders anticipated the release of US inflation data this week for clues about the Fed's monetary policy at the upcoming meeting, while they continued to monitor Strait of Hormuz developments. Materials, utilities, consumer discretionary, and real estate mainly drove the index. Contact Energy rose 1.1% after posting a 27.8% jump in net profit, following the acquisition of Manawa Energy, completed in July 2025. Other early gainers included South Port NZ (0.8%), Ebos Group (0.7%), and Port of Tauranga (0.7%).

Rezaei led the IRGC from 1981–1997 and has served as secretary of the Expediency Council. Both men are hardliners and former IRGC commanders; it is unclear how the changes will alter Tehran’s negotiating stance. Political sources said Zolghadr’s resignation was unrelated to security and that his conditions for reopening the strait align with Mujtaba’s views and must be met for talks to proceed. 【Analyst: RBA likely to hold at 4.35% with hawkish bias; watch persistent inflation】Economists and traders expect the Reserve Bank of Australia to keep the cash rate at 4.35% at Tuesday’s meeting — a second consecutive hold — while reiterating readiness to tighten further to combat persistent inflation. Markets will focus on the policy statement and updated quarterly forecasts for signals on whether the RBA has finished hiking after 75bp of moves this year or intends further tightening. Carl Ang, fixed income research at MFS Investment Management, says a hawkish pause is the base case but slow disinflation means additional tightening cannot be ruled out. Economists broadly expect an extended pause through the remainder of this year and most of next; Commonwealth Bank’s Belinda Allen expects the RBA to bring forward the forecast for inflation falling below 3% and to lift its unemployment projection.

【Musk signals possible free-electron laser challenge to ASML's EUV】SpaceX said it will partner with Tesla on an initial $16.8 bln investment to build a Terafab chip facility in Texas. Posts and a blogger’s read of Terafab materials suggest the plant may adopt free-electron laser (FEL) technology as a lithography light source to challenge ASML’s LPP EUV; Musk replied with FEL FTW and the facility layout reportedly supports that reading. FEL proponents cite higher power, coherence, tunable wavelength and potentially better energy efficiency, and in theory it could push lithography wavelengths from 13.5nm toward ~6nm or shorter. Commercialisation hurdles remain significant: large accelerator systems, capital cost and production stability are the main obstacles.

### 2026-08-10 04:47:28  #其他

【基金投顧牌照擴容進入衝刺期 內外資公募加緊佈局謀轉型】距離8月末基金投顧牌照申報時點日漸臨近，基金投顧新一輪擴容已然進入衝刺階段。證券時報記者多方採訪瞭解到，本次申報陣營涵蓋券商、內資公募以及外資公募等多類市場主體，各家機構正緊鑼密鼓打磨申報材料，系統改造搭建、內部配套制度建設、迎接現場檢查的各項籌備工作全面鋪開，大量工作需要跨部門協同推進。 自2019年啟動試點至今，基金投顧業務走過近七年發展歷程，行業規模、客戶基數持續壯大，而本輪牌照擴容被業內普遍看作基金投顧從試點執行邁向常態化規範發展的關鍵轉折點。伴隨監管立法提速、行業人才培育體系加快構建，再疊加AI技術在客戶分層、資產配置、投資者陪伴領域的深度落地，行業期待借本次擴容契機，破解價值感知弱、收費模式單一、潛在利益衝突等現實堵點，真正推動國內買方投顧轉型走深走實。（證券時報）

【木頭姐發文： 就業資料未顯衰退，AI正驅動美國經濟進入通縮與高生產率新週期 】針對近期引發市場擔憂的美國就業資料，美國知名投資機構方舟投資（ARK Invest）創始人凱西·伍德（Cathie Wood）8日表示，表面上的就業疲軟掩蓋了深層的經濟結構性變化。她指出，人工智慧（AI）等顛覆性技術正在大幅提升美國企業的生產率，當前美國經濟面臨的最大風險並非通貨膨脹，而是技術通縮。伍德強調，美國當前的宏觀經濟指標正呈現出類似20世紀80年代“里根經濟學”時期的特徵。她指出，美國國內企業稅前利潤佔國內生產總值（GDP）的比重已達13.2%，創下數十年來的新高。這一高利潤水平不僅源於疫情期間的財政與貨幣刺激，更得益於企業透過AI和自動化技術大幅提升了生產效率。她預計，隨著生產率的加速提升，美國聯邦赤字佔GDP的比重有望在年底前降至5%左右。

【中國創新藥企邁入價值兌現期：創新藥納保開啟確定性增長通道】兩年前，康方生物實驗室裡走出的依沃西單抗注射液（下稱“依沃西”）在國際頭對頭Ⅲ期臨床中擊敗全球原“藥王”K藥（帕博利珠單抗）。海外醫藥圈評論稱，中國生物製藥行業正在迎來“DeepSeek時刻”。 兩年過去，依沃西的兩款適應證已先後進入國家基本醫保目錄。在廣東某縣域，確診肺癌晚期的張大爺年事已高，受不了頻繁進城住院的折騰和高企的費用，也難耐傳統含化療方案的毒性。醫保政策一落地，他選了依沃西這套“去化療”一線方案，每療程自費壓到2000元上下。 從國際頂刊的學術認可，到一張普通患者的藥費清單，這是本土創新藥價值兌現的一個縮影。 近年來，創新藥從獲批到進入醫保目錄的週期已由原先的數年壓縮至1年左右，約80%的創新藥可在上市兩年內納入醫保支付範圍。一個正被反覆驗證的行業共識是：一旦國內創新藥的支付瓶頸得以打通，產品便會進入銷售放量期，並逐步轉化為持續的營收增長。今年以來，多家頭部創新藥企已憑藉自研管線實質兌現商業化收益。 但與此同時，“一個支付方”的博弈終究有邊界，本土市場的規模天花板也客觀存在。在藉助醫保解決本土患者可及性問題的同時，中國創新藥企仍需在全球市場上尋找更高維度的價值兌現場。（一財）

【美財長貝森特多舉措干預債市，華爾街擔憂“賣出美國”情緒升溫】美國財政部長貝森特近日採取聯合干預日元匯率、調整債券發行指導方針等一系列非常規舉措，試圖遏制美國長期國債收益率的飆升。然而，受財政赤字擴大、通脹高企及政策不確定性等多重因素影響，華爾街對美債市場的信心出現動儘管美國財政部動用多種工具試圖壓低借貸成本，但市場對其實際效果持謹慎態度。當前，受中東地緣衝突推高能源成本、美國政府年度預算赤字逼近2萬億美元等因素影響，30年期美國國債收益率已升至5%以上，創下2007年以來的最高水平。部分華爾街機構指出，貝森特與美聯儲在政策溝通上的不確定性構成了“雙重打擊”，導致投資者開始重新評估對美國核心資產的偏好。在此背景下，“賣出美國”交易策略在全球機構投資者中重新受到關注。該策略的核心是減持美股、美債和美元。分析人士警告，如果美國不能有效降低通脹預期並削減財政赤字，僅靠短期的外匯干預和債務期限調整難以從根本上扭轉市場信心。隨著全球資金開始重新定價美國資產，美元在未來12個月內可能面臨進一步下行壓力，美國債務市場的結構性挑戰正日益凸顯。搖，全球資本市場“賣出美國”的交易情緒再度升溫。

【WSJ: US officials say Trump patient on Iran standoff, prioritizing Strait of Hormuz shipping】The Wall Street Journal reported US officials say President Trump is taking a patient approach to the Iran standoff and expects to weather it, particularly while gasoline prices remain near current levels. A White House official said US strikes have hit all identified Iranian military targets. Trump’s immediate priority is keeping energy and other shipping through the Strait of Hormuz open; officials say the US will retain military options if Iran mounts further attacks on vessels. Officials added Trump has privately told senior aides in recent weeks that US strikes last year destroyed three major Iranian nuclear facilities and that Iran is unlikely to restart its nuclear program during his term; he believes US intelligence would detect any rebuilding and that the threat of further strikes would serve as ongoing deterrence. Officials said if the US can contain Iran’s nuclear program and the Strait reopens, Trump is more likely to extend the current ceasefire indefinitely, and that if Iran fully reopens the Strait he would lift the US military blockade of Iranian ports.

### 2026-08-09 21:03:45  #市場 #觀點

【槓桿ETF規模激增放大市場波動，華爾街日內動量交易迎爆發式增長】隨著槓桿ETF資產規模的爆炸式增長，其每日機械性再平衡機制正顯著放大市場波動，進而為華爾街日內動量交易策略創造了新的獲利途徑。當前，半導體等科技板塊已成為此類短期趨勢交易的核心領域。槓桿ETF的運作機制決定了其具有天然的順週期特徵。為維持既定槓桿倍數，此類基金在市場上漲時必須追加買入，下跌時則被迫賣出。在此背景下，專注於高波動資產的日內動量策略迎來了顯著增長。資料顯示，自2024年以來，針對半導體行業的日內動量有效策略數量增長逾兩倍。這類策略透過嚴格的量化規則在價格大幅波動時捕捉短期趨勢，其風險調整後收益遠超美股整體指數表現。摩根大通策略師指出，當前市場的高波動性特徵令人回想起1998年技術革命時期，為日內動量策略提供了極佳的交易環境。

【美國“外行任大使”現象惹質疑】美國《華盛頓郵報》近期梳理了本屆美國總統任期內已任命或提名的駐外大使名單，發現其中絕大多數要麼是助選的“金主”，要麼是政治方面的“忠實擁躉”。該報認為，這種做法違背了數十年來主要從職業外交官中挑選駐外大使的美國傳統。《華盛頓郵報》6日發表的這篇報道援引美國外交人員協會的資料，比較了自福特總統之後歷任美國總統任內國會中期選舉年份的駐外大使崗位情況。資料顯示，截至今年7月中旬的本屆美國政府任期內，在任大使90人，其中僅25人為職業外交官出身，佔比不到三分之一，其餘65人屬於“政治任命”；尚有102個大使崗位空缺，超過崗位總數一半，其中72個崗位未提名人選，另外30名大使候選人正等待國會參議院批准。（新華社）

### 2026-08-09 20:10:18  #其他

【瑞典新研究：RNA預處理可助提升胰島移植效率】瑞典卡羅琳醫學院的研究人員最新開發出一種靶向核糖核酸（RNA）技術，可在胰島移植前對胰島細胞進行短暫預處理，使其移植後能更快地重建血液供應，並有望減少移植所需的供體胰島數量。對糖尿病小鼠進行的實驗證實，與移植未經處理胰島的小鼠相比，移植預處理胰島的小鼠體內，存活並能保持功能的胰島β細胞數量更多，血糖控制情況也更好。研究人員認為，如果進一步研究證實安全有效，這種RNA預處理技術有望提高有限供體胰島的利用效率，使更多患者受益。此外，該技術也有可能用於改善其他型別的細胞移植效果。相關研究成果已發表在國際學術期刊《訊號轉導與靶向治療》上。（新華社）

【CITIC Securities: Downside likely priced in, supporting extended Hong Kong equity gains】CITIC Securities says the Hang Seng Composite Index has seen an earnings-expectations reversal over the past month; interim results beats and positive profit guidance have driven upward revisions to full-year earnings. The Hang Seng TECH index is lagging, hit by divergent passenger-car profitability and higher capex at leading internet platforms that compress near-term margins. Sector outlook: upgrades for healthcare (CXO and large-cap pharma), financials (broker asset management and insurers), utilities and cyclical transport; downgrades for consumer, property and information technology. Flow pattern is two-pronged: capital rotating into oversold low-base sectors while momentum trades target names with strong earnings trajectories. Against a dense earnings calendar and global macro volatility, the firm favors a dividend-defense plus growth-optionalilty barbell: defensive allocation to high-dividend, low-beta, bond-like stocks; offensive exposure to internet giants, robotics and biotech benefiting from two-way capital flows, and tech hardware/AI applications, with selective positions in innovative drugs and industrial metals for near-term catalysts.

【CITIC Securities: US-Japan FX intervention aims to curb yen-depreciation spillovers】CITIC Securities said US-Japan joint FX intervention aims to prevent spillovers from sustained yen weakness. Japan’s inflation remains below the BOJ target, limiting willingness to tighten policy; Sanae Takaichi’s proposed tax cuts could widen Japan’s fiscal gap and erode confidence in yen assets. The US is concerned that yen stabilization or Japanese reductions in US Treasury holdings could, amid elevated US debt supply, push up long-end US yields. Short-term intervention should help stabilize expectations, but with US-Japan rate differentials still wide the scope for a sustained large yen rally is limited. The report adds that US equities retain clearer advantages over Japanese stocks on earnings growth, industry structure and the AI supply chain.

【俄烏互襲致多人死傷】俄羅斯別爾哥羅德州代理州長亞歷山大·舒瓦耶夫9日在社交媒體上說，過去24小時內，烏克蘭軍隊對該州發動了146次襲擊，造成3人死亡、25人受傷。 舒瓦耶夫說，襲擊還造成部分車輛和房屋起火，大量行政和商業設施受損。俄防空部隊過去24小時擊落了162架無人機。 俄國防部9日釋出戰報稱，俄軍繼續對烏克蘭的港口和船隻進行打擊，其中包括敖德薩港和切爾諾莫斯克港用於裝卸和儲存燃料、軍用物資等的設施。 烏克蘭總統澤連斯基9日在社交媒體發文說，8日晚至9日凌晨，俄軍對敖德薩、哈爾科夫、第聶伯羅彼得羅夫斯克、基輔等多地發動大規模空襲，烏港口設施和多地居民樓、購物中心、能源設施受損。截至9日上午，襲擊造成至少2人死亡，另有數十人受傷。 澤連斯基說，過去一週，俄軍向烏境內發射了61枚導彈，其中56枚為彈道導彈，此外還發射了1560架無人機以及1540枚航空炸彈。(新華社)

### 2026-08-09 18:07:23  #國際

【高市早苗再度對無核三原則表態含糊】日本長崎遭原子彈轟炸81週年紀念儀式今天（8月9日）在長崎市和平紀念公園舉行。日本首相高市早苗參加儀式並致辭。有日本專家指出，高市致辭時對“無核三原則”仍含糊表態，未明確表示日本今後將繼續堅持該原則。有日本專家表示，此次高市早苗的措辭與歷任日本首相略有不同，且未明確表示日本將繼續堅持該原則，這可能為了年末修改“安保三檔案”留下了空間。日本在野黨此前針對高市6日在廣島遭原子彈轟炸81週年紀念儀式上的致辭表示，高市的表述僅僅是說明現狀，缺乏今後繼續堅持“無核三原則”的決心。 （CCTV國際時訊）

【累計貢獻油氣超600萬噸 我國海上深層潛山油氣開發能力顯著提升】今天（8月9日），記者從中國海油獲悉，我國渤海首個千億方大氣田——渤中19-6氣田一期開發專案全面投產，日產油氣當量超5200噸。隨著渤中19-6、渤中26-6等深層潛山油氣藏相繼投入開發，我國海上深層潛山累計貢獻油氣產量超600萬噸，開發能力實現顯著提升。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-09 18:00:14  #其他

【胡塞武裝宣佈對沙特軍事目標發動大規模打擊】也胡塞武裝8日宣佈，對沙特在葉門西部沿海地區穆哈附近的軍事集結和武器倉庫發動大規模軍事行動，使用彈道導彈和無人機對目標實施精確打擊，造成大量裝備損毀和數十名沙特方面人員傷亡。胡塞武裝發表宣告稱，此次行動是對沙特持續向葉門西部沿海及代爾省地區增派兵力、調運武器裝備並實施軍事打擊的回應。宣告指出，葉門方面將堅持"以封鎖對封鎖、以升級對升級"的原則，持續監視並精確打擊任何針對葉門人民和自由省份的軍事集結。宣告強調，葉門武裝部隊不會允許沙特方面透過打擊葉門民眾來推進其所謂"陰謀議程"，並將繼續跟蹤和打擊沙特的軍事動向。

【白宮官員證實特朗普與美聯儲主席定期通話 強調尊重央行獨立性】近日，美國白宮國家經濟委員會主任凱文·哈西特在接受採訪時證實，美國總統特朗普與新任美聯儲主席凱文·沃什經常通話討論經濟問題。哈西特稱，特朗普尊重美聯儲的獨立性，並確信特朗普不會就利率決策向美聯儲主席施壓。據悉，特朗普與沃什的定期通話在白宮與美聯儲的關係史上實屬罕見，這引發了人們對總統謀求影響美聯儲決策的擔憂。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-09 17:56:43  #其他

【青島港單月岸電接電量重新整理歷史紀錄】據青島港公眾號，今年以來，山東港口青島港QQCTU持續最佳化岸電運營管控模式、精進作業服務、升級智慧管控體系，岸電接電量同比增長30.01%，實現階段性穩步增長。其中，7月岸電作業成效尤為突出，單月接電量同比提升3.8%、環比提升15.4%，成功重新整理碼頭單月岸電接電量歷史紀錄。船舶岸電是港口綠色轉型的重要抓手，也是港區節能減汙降碳、最佳化生態環境、落實“雙碳”戰略的關鍵舉措。

【新疆釋出高溫橙色預警 區域性氣溫超45℃】新疆維吾爾自治區氣象臺8月9日15時32分發布高溫橙色預警訊號：預計9日下午至19日，天山北坡、南疆盆地、東疆大部和塔城地區北部、阿勒泰地區西部的部分地區將有37℃以上的高溫天氣，其中博州東部、塔城地區北部、克拉瑪依市、石河子市、昌吉州、阿克蘇地區、巴州、吐魯番市、哈密市等地的部分割槽域最高氣溫將升至40℃以上，吐魯番市區域性區域將達45℃以上。需加強防範山區局地融雪型洪水，並注意防暑降溫和用火安全。（央視新聞）
